# Codex handoff

## Current task

- Implement the Connector boundary/resource refactor program through PR-CX00..PR-BG01 as root integrator, using three non-overlapping parallel lanes.
- Status: Waves 0, 1, and 2 plus Wave 3 PR-AU01 (CX00/ST01/MC01/UI01/SC01/SV01/MP01/AU01 plus contract parity) are implemented, root-integrated, and green on `codex/connector-refactor`, which started from clean `main@ef4abf01996a5261b91f475d0f740b0d50a4cc26`. Pre-IN01 contract/host parity and the atomic app cutover are next.

## Working area

- Root-only choke points: root `Cargo.toml`, `Cargo.lock`, `xtask`, `crates/app/src/app.rs`, `crates/app/src/main.rs`, `crates/app/src/ui/mod.rs`, and this handoff.
- Target crates: new `connector-contract`, `connector-ui`, `connector-service`; existing `storage`, `mcp-store`, `audit`, `mcp`, `mcp-proxy`, `auth`, `secret`, `runtime`, and `app`.
- Non-negotiable gates: no new boundary allowlist, no Connector variant in `RuntimeCommand`, no Tokio/new async runtime, no long-lived dual production path, no leaf render-path I/O, and no unbounded job/result/secret/diagnostic retention.
- Release Scenario A-E RSS/CPU/frame-p95 remains a real-hardware measurement gate; automated perf smoke is evidence but not release approval.

## Plan

1. Completed — PR-CX00: froze transport-neutral contracts/resource limits, added workspace/dependency gates, and recorded the performance/failure-injection baseline.
2. Completed, parallel Wave 1 — PR-ST01 storage/audit transactions; PR-MC01 MCP limits/cancellation; PR-UI01 pure virtualized connector UI.
3. Completed, parallel Wave 2 — PR-SC01 secret slots/redaction; PR-SV01 lazy coordinator/latest snapshot; PR-MP01 proxy session reuse.
4. Completed — PR-AU01 shared authorization/audit. In progress — pre-IN01 durable OAuth/event-wake completion and full credential-path audit.
5. Pending, dependencies pulled forward — finish SC01 legacy-cleanup crash ledger, B00b-core RuntimeSecretResolver/RuntimeHost, and the SB01 credential/env snapshot-intent contract before changing any production credential pointer.
6. Pending — pull the CR01 `App::bootstrap`/credential-vault composition seam immediately before root-only PR-IN01, then atomically cut Connector production plus the credential/env path and delete the old Connector implementation.
7. Pending — finish the remaining agents/env 14-exception SB01 slice, B00b remainder, and PR-OD01 diagnostics/soak/log GC.
8. Pending — PR-CR01 remainder and PR-BG01 zero-allowlist/production gates.

- 2026-07-22: The full credential consumer audit found a new IN01 blocker in the committed v28 ledger. It durably tracks newly allocated physical staging/published/orphan slots, but after a legacy logical keyring entry is copied and its credential pointer is published, a crash before deleting the logical source leaves no exact durable cleanup marker. Startup cannot rediscover that source without prohibited platform-wide keychain search or retaining a dual resolver forever. Before IN01, storage needs a bounded exact legacy-cleanup marker/API covering copy/publish/source-delete/ack crash points; the app bootstrap then completes migration and only exposes physical pointers. The audit also found connector-service must best-effort delete+ack an exact staging slot when keyring staging itself fails, otherwise repeated failures can accumulate ledger rows until restart reconciliation.

- 2026-07-22: Root independently verified the authorization global-revision CAS follow-up: storage 111/111, audit 48/48, all-target checks, strict storage/audit/mcp-store Clippy, scoped rustfmt, and diff-check pass. `commit_authorization_preflight_revision_cas` acquires IMMEDIATE, checks the exact durable revision and permission fingerprint, persists remembered permission plus Prepared audit, and returns the final revision in one transaction. - 2026-07-22: Root resolved the remaining keyring source-allocation concern at the design level: production IN01 must not call `Entry::search`, because the platform API allocates an all-match Vec before Rust can apply the 4,096/+1 bound. Since versioned physical slots are not yet active in production, the cutover can avoid this permanently by recording every allocated slot in a bounded SQLite staging/published/orphan ledger before keyring writes, atomically changing ledger state with pointer publication, and reconciling exact ledger IDs at startup. Legacy logical access/refresh/DCR aliases are enumerable from bounded credential rows and need no platform search. A storage migration/API plus crash-window tests must land before the app pointer-index bootstrap; the existing search-based helper may remain for compatibility/tests but cannot be used by the production adapter.

Second, the legacy `complete_audit_operation`/`Db::complete_tool_audit` path matches any Prepared row by operation ID and can finalize an owner-scoped AU row without its scope/run token; it must be deleted at cutover or restricted to owner-NULL legacy rows with a rejection test. Third, `AuthorizationPlan` discards the permission state/revision used for evaluation, so a concurrent Deny between evaluation and preflight can still execute; bind an expected absent/rule/approved-hash fingerprint and revalidate it inside the storage transaction before remembered mutation/audit. Canonical-path DB identity also needs physical file identity plus hard-link/path-replacement tests before claiming exact DB binding. The otherwise-green properties are the fixed 256-stripe bound, same-scope/run recovery, exact owner-scoped outcome idempotency, raw audit blob NULL, exact server/tool/input digest, and redacted Debug. Agent B is authorized to correct these within the existing audit/storage lane before freeze; the red-team made no files changes and ran no cargo command.

MCP production proxy now exposes only the combined AuthorizedToolExecutor, parses tool arguments with borrowed RawValue, owns one non-Clone/non-Serialize/redacted/volatile-zeroizing SensitiveToolInput, has a distinct exact 64-KiB request cap plus 32-KiB tool-input cap, secure buffer growth/read-ahead wiping, and closed sanitized outcomes. - 2026-07-22: IN01 approval-wake design is narrowed to a lazy event-driven lifetime: the existing always-on `ApprovalWatcher` will be deleted; at most one bounded listener may exist only while at least one live agent session is actually configured with the proxy, each proxy wake triggers at most one pending-approval DB read and one coalesced repaint, and the listener/socket is removed when the last such session exits. There is no periodic timeout query and the manual refresh path remains. This preserves zero additional Connector thread/socket/poll/repaint for an ordinary Terminal while still making cross-process approvals visible. Exact IPC implementation will reuse an existing app-owned lifecycle/wake path if practical and must not add a Connector `RuntimeCommand`.

- 2026-07-22: IN01/OD01 resource audit identified a pre-existing always-on `ApprovalWatcher` in `app.rs`: App bootstrap opens a second DB connection, starts an `approval-watcher` thread, and polls all pending approvals on a fixed interval even when no Connector/proxy is used. This directly conflicts with the final zero-unused-thread and zero-idle-DB/polling/repaint criteria. It cannot survive the final cutover. The replacement must be event-driven and lazy (prefer reusing an existing app IPC/wake channel or a bounded listener instantiated only with an active proxy configuration), query once per actual wake, and retain a manual recovery path without periodic polling; exact design is gated for IN01/OD01 after AU's proxy lifecycle freezes.
- 2026-07-22: AU01 owner locking was simplified to a hard filesystem bound: each canonical DB lazily uses one of 256 hash-striped cross-platform `fs2` lock files, so high-cardinality proxy scopes cannot create an unbounded file set and no registry/unlink/cleanup scan is needed. Hash collisions only serialize unrelated scopes conservatively. The owner still contains the exact DB identity, scope, random run UUID, and OS lock for its lifetime. Agent B reverted an accidental root-only Cargo.lock regeneration; root then applied exactly the required `storage` dependency entries for already-cached `fs2` and workspace `sha2` via `apply_patch`, and B will not modify the lockfile further.
- 2026-07-22: AU01 proxy security review rejected using the legacy split `PermissionHook(&Value)` then `ToolForwarder(Value)` production path. That API keeps raw tool arguments in both the parsed JSON value and a serialized string throughout a potentially long approval poll and cannot structurally zeroize them. MCP will add an audit/storage-neutral combined authorized-executor callback that owns a bounded non-Clone/non-Serialize/redacted-Debug zeroizing argument byte type; request bytes and parsed JSON are erased before the blocking callback, the proxy binds the grant to those exact bytes, and only reparses immediately for the one backend call. Sanitized structured non-retryable tool errors replace raw `anyhow` JSON-RPC failures. Required tests observe zeroization on deny/timeout/cancel/error and prove explicit null reaches neither authorization nor call.
- 2026-07-22: AU01 service red-team found a cross-channel event race: result completions are drained before command intents, so an already-dispatched Cancel or config/policy mutation can lose to a schema completion and allow preflight/external call. The fix must be dispatch-visible rather than merely reversing loop order: cancellation is set through a bounded shared active-operation token registry before enqueue, and internal command envelopes carry a monotonic mutation epoch captured by jobs and checked on completion. Deterministic schema-completion races must prove preflight/call count zero. A separate exact-target regression requires every `load_server(A)` consumer to reject a returned draft B before schema/network work.

- 2026-07-22: AU01 owner-recovery review rejected the first independent `AuthorizationGeneration` draft. A generation could outlive its OS scope lock and race a new same-scope executor; it also trusted callers not to pair an owner acquired for one DB with another DB, and its generation registry/per-scope lock names could grow with high-cardinality proxy scopes. The corrected API must expose one non-Clone active owner that itself retains the cross-platform exclusive lock and a private per-run UUID, bind that owner to the exact DB identity, and require it for preflight/completion. Beginning a run reconciles only same-scope/different-run Prepared rows, eliminating the persistent generation registry; lock-file cleanup must be inode-race safe and bounded. Required regressions cover live-owner exclusion, post-drop takeover, cross-scope isolation, stale/cross-DB owner rejection, and operation-id non-reuse.
- 2026-07-22: AU01 transport red-team found that the new typed stdio server response error changed its display text while MP01 still string-matched `server error:` to decide whether a lease was poisoned. That would reconnect a healthy warm backend after a known server/tool error. MCP and proxy must share a stable typed classification/downcast: known server responses preserve the lease, while delivery/transport ambiguity poisons it; concrete stdio timeout/EOF/protocol/cancel-after-send tests must each prove one external call and Unknown/no retry.
- 2026-07-22: AU01 red-team found the prior global Prepared crash sweep unsafe: GUI and multiple proxy processes share one DB, so any startup could mark another live call Unknown. The replacement design is scoped generation guarded by an exclusive OS lifetime lock, not a global or age-only sweep and not a heartbeat. Storage will acquire a nonblocking per-DB/per-scope Unix lock, then atomically advance that scope generation and reconcile only its prior generations; a live same-scope executor prevents takeover, crash releases the lock, and other scopes remain untouched. Proxy uses `proxy:{pane-or-explicit-owner}:{server}` and fails closed without either pane ID or `DEPPY_AUTHORIZATION_OWNER`; the IN01 app repository will lazily hold a stable GUI-scope lease only while the connector worker/repository is active. Required tests cover live A vs B startup, dead A takeover, cross-scope isolation, and operation-ID non-reuse.
- 2026-07-22: AU01 red-team also found stdio delivery ambiguity was not typed: after a tools/call write, stdio timeout/EOF/protocol violation/cancel currently becomes generic Failed while HTTP becomes `McpDeliveryUnknown`. The proxy must fetch live schema on every call over its warm connection (not retain a config-revision-only schema cache), because same-connection dynamic schema changes must trigger reapproval.

The corrected full proxy run passed 28/30; only the two existing localhost `TcpListener::bind` fixtures failed with sandbox EPERM, while an immediately preceding full run of the same functional code passed 30/30. Strict Clippy did not run in the chained command after those test failures and is being rerun separately.

MP01 is changing target snapshots to clone only `Arc<BackendConfig>`. Pre-existing `mcp::McpServerConfig`/`McpHttpServerConfig` are also secret-bearing and Clone (the latter is used by legacy `connectors.rs`); their Clone implementations must be removed atomically with PR-IN01 legacy deletion so production ends with zero Clone secret-bearing config types.

- 2026-07-22: PR-MC01 completed in `crates/mcp`. Discovery now caps cumulative tools at 4,096 and serialized descriptor/cursor metadata at 8 MiB with cursor-cycle detection. Tool input is capped at 32 KiB before spawn/connect, and raw JSON/SSE response input is capped at 8 MiB. Stdio timeout/cancel kills the process group, reaps the child, and joins owned pipe threads. Sync HTTP sends, including caller-timed-out sends, retain one of two permits until actual completion and use a bounded two-handle opportunistic reaper with no resident thread. `tools/call` session-expiry/header-timeout returns `McpDeliveryUnknown` and is never automatically retried. Public transport metrics include stdout/stderr/writer/HTTP helper threads and permit/reaper state. Focused tests, `cargo check -p mcp`, strict Clippy, scoped fmt, and diff check pass. A full listener-capable run passed 77/78 before correcting the sole obsolete 64 KiB assertion; the corrected test passes. The later full run passed 49/78 with all remaining 29 failures caused by sandbox `TcpListener::bind: Operation not permitted`. Next: unrestricted full MCP rerun, then SV01 consumes cancellation/metrics and AU01 maps `McpDeliveryUnknown` to durable audit `Unknown` without retry.

- 2026-07-19: 이번 요청 구현과 최종 읽기 전용 점검을 마쳤다. source font assets는 D2Coding Regular 4,185,844 bytes + JetBrains 5 weights 1,360,972 bytes = 5,546,816 bytes이며 D2Coding Bold 4,359,956 bytes는 삭제됐다. 사이드바용 AppleSDGothicNeo.ttc는 55,373,848 bytes(52.81MiB) 시스템 리소스를 한 번 읽고 실행 파일에는 포함하지 않는다. 실제 사용자 로그 디렉터리는 아직 797,892KiB(779.19MiB)다. 사용자가 빌드·실행을 금지했으므로 기존 로그/실행 프로세스는 건드리지 않았고, 새 소스 앱의 다음 시작 때 파일별 상한+전체 256MiB GC가 적용된다. `git diff --check`, D2 Bold 파일 부재와 소스 참조 부재가 통과했다. 앱 패키징·재실행·커밋은 하지 않았다.

- 2026-07-16: GitHub verification found the exact upstream root cause and a proposed fix. Alacritty issues `#6942` (Korean composition followed by dropped punctuation/ASCII) and `#8079` (one Korean-IME Space produces two spaces) remain open and are labeled macOS/winit dependency defects. winit PR `#4478` (`macOS: Fix Korean composing text commit key handling`) explicitly fixes both: macOS may call `insertText` twice inside one `interpretKeyEvents`; winit 0.30.13 leaves `marked_text` stale until after the whole call, so the second callback sees composition still active and can emit a duplicate Commit, while a `Committed` early return drops the triggering ASCII command. The PR clears marked text immediately, forwards post-commit trigger characters as regular key input, and removes the overbroad early return. Local inspection confirms the unpatched code exists verbatim in cached winit 0.30.13 (`src/platform_impl/macos/view.rs:410-430`). The PR is still open with one small 12-addition/8-deletion commit, no reviews/comments, and targets winit 0.31.0-beta.2; the latest stable and this app's resolved dependency are both 0.30.13, whose March release does not contain it. Recommended implementation is a reproducible minimal backport into a locally patched/vendored 0.30.13 rather than pointing eframe at the incompatible 0.31 beta. Once source-level events are corrected, reassess/remove the overlapping punctuation key-up heuristic to avoid compensating twice. The egui 0.33 focus changes (`#7827` and `Context::text_edit_focused`) are already included in this app's egui 0.35, so the Agents→PTY focus defect remains local focus arbitration rather than a missing egui upgrade.

- 2026-07-16: Remediation started after the user approved proceeding. Before changing renderer configuration, dependency source inspection found that `eframe::NativeOptions::default()` already sets Wgpu `SurfaceConfig::LOW_LATENCY` on native builds; the earlier audit had looked only at `egui_wgpu::WgpuConfiguration::default()` and therefore overstated the drawable-buffering opportunity. Metal latency tuning was removed from the implementation plan. Production still unnecessarily enables Glow for the renderer benchmark, so only that feature separation remains in renderer scope.

- 2026-07-16: Follow-up memory review corrected the earlier causal overstatement. The old debug executable's +34.5% measurement is only isolated `pre_run_native` RSS; macOS RSS includes resident clean mappings, while Activity Monitor's app-memory/footprint view is centered on dirty and compressed memory, so file size, in-app RSS, and Activity Monitor are not directly interchangeable. A same-state full-GUI `footprint`/`vmmap` comparison was attempted but blocked because the sandboxed native process exited before inspection and the tools reported permission/process-lifetime failures. Confirmed two product issues: `activity_summary` adds the same whole-app PID RSS once per active/warm runtime, and the 128MB "global" terminal cache budget is broadcast unchanged to each runtime and enforced only against that runtime's sessions (three resident runtimes can therefore retain roughly 384MB of estimated terminal cache before per-runtime archival pressure). Ordinary non-exited shells also mark a warm workspace live, bypassing `max_warm` and 30-minute eviction until the RAM-derived `max_live_warm` limit of 3–8. These latter two can increase real memory when restored sessions/scrollback are populated; Activity duplication only inflates the displayed total. Dependency inspection confirms the app links only `wgpu 29.0.4`, not multiple wgpu versions. Production nevertheless enables both Wgpu and Glow for benchmark switching, and egui-wgpu defaults to Metal high-throughput surface buffering; those are lower-confidence optimization targets, not established causes of the cross-Mac 50% report. No product source was modified.

- 2026-07-16: Diagnosed the reported other-Mac 50% memory increase. The root `Deppy-Sijo-0.1.0-arm64.zip` accepted earlier by Gmail embeds a 110,034,880-byte development/debug `deppy-sijo`, whereas `target/bundle/Deppy-Sijo-0.1.0-arm64.zip` embeds the 38,075,312-byte optimized release binary. Three isolated self-RSS samples per artifact at `pre_run_native` averaged 23.06MiB for the old package and 17.15MiB for release, making the old package 34.5% higher before renderer/UI allocation. The Activity summary has a separate accounting defect: every in-process workspace monitor reports `std::process::id()` and whole-process RSS, while `activity_summary` sums every workspace snapshot and then all session-child RSS, so two live workspaces can duplicate the app RSS and agent processes intentionally expand the displayed boundary. No product source was modified in this diagnostic turn. Current signed bundle executable SHA-256 is `3da61c6dab9fb9add274708a10634b753d6301b2dcc3282bb4475b36b70d6a55`; old debug bundle executable SHA-256 is `96be224ef0a02eaa855c4265e7a291cec2982637be62cd25c1eeaadc4e36cbc4`.

- 2026-07-16: Investigated another-Mac terminal output rendering Korean as literal `???`. Both D2Coding faces are compiled with `include_bytes!` into `deppy-sijo`, so the terminal does not depend on AppleGothic being installed and the app bundle is not missing a terminal font resource. Reproduced the portability boundary locally: a macOS zsh started with a Finder-like empty environment reports `US-ASCII`, while setting `LANG=en_US.UTF-8` reports `UTF-8`. The PTY backend currently sets terminal capability variables but no locale fallback, so GUI launch environment differences can make shell/agent programs replace Korean with question marks before rendering.

- 2026-07-16: Investigated intermittent image clipboard paste requiring a second Command+V. Current egui-winit consumes a recognized paste key-down after attempting a text-only clipboard read; image-only pasteboards therefore produce neither `Event::Paste` nor the original pressed `Event::Key`. The local workspace compensated by detecting only the later V key-up, leaving the first gesture vulnerable to focus/modifier loss. OpenAI Codex #3397/#10523 and Ghostty discussion #10478 report the same macOS image-only Cmd+V gap; Ghostty PR #11571 proposed the same PNG/TIFF-to-temp-path conversion already present locally, while Claude Code's changelog confirms direct Cmd+V mapping as the successful trigger-side fix.

- 2026-07-15: Hardened the whole terminal-agent typing path for Codex/Claude. Printable punctuation arrives from egui-winit as paired `Key` + `Text` events; the mapper now has a regression test proving `.`, `,`, `/`, `?`, `!`, and space write exactly once. More importantly, pane click/search-close/focus-cycle now records an optimistic `pending_focus` input target before the async runtime `MuxUpdated` response, so the first printable key cannot be dropped or routed to the prior pane. A pending terminal refocus also overrides one-frame stale hidden `TextEdit` focus, while visible popups/windows still block terminal input. Verified `git diff --check`, `cargo check -p deppy-sijo`, `cargo test -p deppy-sijo ui::workspace::tests` (19 passed), `cargo test -p terminal input_mapper::tests` (11 passed), `cargo test -p terminal` (56 passed), and `cargo build -p deppy-sijo`.

- 2026-07-15: Fixed the remaining Korean IME special-character gap. During an active Korean composition, macOS/egui can emit a printable `Key` plus `Ime::Commit` but omit `Text`; the terminal mapper previously ignored the Key to prevent normal Key/Text duplication, so `.` and other symbols were lost on their first press. Added a restricted ASCII fallback for punctuation, shifted symbols, digits, and space only while an IME batch is involved. Any matching Text/Commit removes exactly one fallback candidate, so normal input and repeated symbols remain single-entry; Ctrl/Command/Option shortcuts are never synthesized. Added regression coverage for missing-Text, normal Key/Text, and repeated punctuation. Verified `cargo test -p terminal input_mapper::tests` (13 passed), `cargo test -p deppy-sijo ui::workspace::tests` (22 passed), `cargo build -p deppy-sijo`, scoped `rustfmt` for `input_mapper.rs`, and `git diff --check`. Refreshed and ad-hoc signed the `target/bundle/Deppy Sijo.app` bundle; signature verification passes. Restart is still required to load the new binary.
- 2026-07-15: The user confirmed the first `.` after a Korean jamo was still lost. Root cause was deeper than the prior same-frame fallback: winit 0.30 macOS intentionally suppresses a printable key-down when `interpretKeyEvents` commits marked text, then emits only that key's key-up after IME state returns to Ground. Added a short-lived, session-bound pending IME release state. An IME Commit arms it; the next printable key-up restores the swallowed ASCII character. Commit/Text and same-frame fallback characters are tracked so an already-delivered symbol is never duplicated; pure modifier releases are ignored so Shift+symbols remain recoverable, while other non-printable releases consume the state. Regression tests cover `ㅁ` + swallowed Period key-down + Period key-up, Commit already containing `.`, same-frame fallback deduplication, and modifier-release ordering. Verified `cargo test -p terminal` (59 passed), `cargo test -p deppy-sijo ui::workspace::tests` (25 passed), `cargo check -p deppy-sijo`, `cargo build -p deppy-sijo`, and `git diff --check`.

The current key-up fallback therefore matches the observed mechanism but remains sensitive to later focus/frame state. Remaining plan changed to capture the original macOS `NSEvent` key-down before winit IME suppression, reconcile it with egui Text/Commit events to avoid duplicates, retain key-up recovery only as a final fallback, then run focused/full tests and rebuild the signed app bundle.

The older session-bound key-up recovery remains as a final fallback and is deduplicated against native recovery.

- 2026-07-16: The new self-RSS test passes. Added a `pre_run_native` stage and captured five isolated WGPU release runs for current and baseline `169a888`. Baseline pre-GUI RSS was 18,137,088–18,153,472 bytes; current was 18,137,088–18,268,160 bytes, overlapping within 0–114,688 bytes. Mach-O used sections increased only about 19KB. Therefore the IME state (bounded 64-key queue) and TOML/muda upgrades do not explain the reported MB-scale runtime increase.
- 2026-07-16: Found and fixed the material font-memory waste. On macOS the default UI font and CJK fallback both point to `/System/Library/Fonts/Supplemental/AppleGothic.ttf` (15,255,648 bytes), but `install_cjk_fallback` read/stored it twice under `cjk` and `ui`. `App::new` also initialized font-change snapshots to sentinels, rebuilding the full definitions again on the first frame even though main had already installed the same settings. The definitions now keep one cached `Arc<FontData>` and use `cjk` as the proportional primary when paths match; repeated definitions reuse the same Arc, and App snapshots start from the installed config. Guaranteed raw-font reduction is 15,255,648 bytes steady-state and at least 45,766,944 bytes of avoided first-frame duplicate allocation/ownership pressure. Font sharing tests (3/3), the startup snapshot regression test (1/1), RSS sampler test, and `git diff --check` pass. Next: run IME/app regressions, clippy/release package/sign, then record the final bundle result.
- 2026-07-16: Post-fix regressions pass: terminal 60/60, workspace/IME 35/35, config 18/18, and focused font/startup/RSS tests. The full app suite completed 239 passed, 6 failed, 2 ignored; all six failures are the existing sandbox denial of localhost OAuth mock binds (`Operation not permitted`), while the formerly sandbox-failing RSS test now passes through `proc_pidinfo`. Strict app clippy passed with only the previously documented unrelated `while_immutable_condition` allow, release build passed, and `git diff --check` is clean. Remaining: official package/signature verification and final worktree review.
- 2026-07-16: Official `scripts/package-macos.sh` completed after the RAM fix; after restoring an unrelated rustfmt-only line, the final exact-source optimized rebuild completed in 1m13s, recreated `target/bundle/Deppy Sijo.app`, ad-hoc signed the bundle, and `codesign --verify --deep --strict --verbose=2` passed. The user's already-running old process must be quit and reopened to load the lower-memory binary; this agent sandbox still cannot control that desktop LaunchServices session reliably.
- 2026-07-19: Removed gitignore-based filtering from the file tree (`crates/app/src/ui/file_tree.rs`). `FileTreeUi` no longer owns `GitIgnoreCache`; listing and watcher now behave like Finder and ignore only the hard-coded generated-dir list (`.git`, `node_modules`, `target`, `dist`, `build`, `.next`, `.turbo`, `vendor`, `logs`, `.cache`, `.DS_Store`). Deleted `GitIgnoreCache`, `IgnoreRule`, and related helpers. Merged `watch_events_for_path_with_ignore` into `watch_events_for_path` as the production function. Updated two tests to assert gitignored entries are visible and generated dirs remain hidden. `cargo check -p deppy-sijo` and `cargo test -p deppy-sijo file_tree` pass (54/54).

- 2026-07-19: Removed the hard-coded generated-directory filter from the file tree (`crates/app/src/ui/file_tree.rs`). `read_children_guarded`, `watch_events_for_path`, and `sync_watches` no longer call `has_default_watch_ignore_component`, so `.git`, `node_modules`, `target`, `dist`, `build`, `.next`, `.turbo`, `vendor`, `logs`, `.cache`, and `.DS_Store` are now listed and watched like Finder. Deleted `has_default_watch_ignore_component` and `default_watch_ignore_name`. Caller-provided `watch_ignore` prefixes remain unchanged for app data/log self-loop prevention. Updated tests: `watcher_generated_경로도_이제_이벤트를_생성한다` asserts generated paths produce events, `gitignore_규칙은_listing에서_더_이상_적용되지_않는다` now asserts `node_modules` is visible, and `watcher_gitignore_rules는_dirty_event를_더_이상_버리지_않는다` now asserts `node_modules` events are kept. `cargo check -p deppy-sijo`, `cargo test -p deppy-sijo file_tree` (54/54), and `cargo clippy -p deppy-sijo --all-targets` all pass.
